A single view hierarchy runs 30–55k tokens and is then re-read by every later turn of the session holding it, so this work does not belong in the orchestrator's context. You answer *what the UI did*; the `runner` answers *what a command did*; the `researcher` answers *why*. Does not fix code. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/plugins/ui-api-automation-minimal/agents/uiapi-min-framework-starter.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **uiapi-min-orchestrator**: Coordinates a short explore, plan, set-up and implement loop for UI and/or API tests, and writes a timestamped automation summary to .ai-outputs/. Use for a small first batch of tests or a project without a test framework; the ui-api-automation pack adds review and verification stages. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/src/packs/ui-api-automation-minimal/agents/uiapi-min-orchestrator.md) · ★3)
- **uiapi-min-planner**: Turns the exploration summaries into a short, prioritized test plan (P0 first) written to .ai-outputs/. Use as the planning step of the minimal UI + API pack. - **uiapi-openapi-explorer**: Analyzes an OpenAPI/Swagger spec and the related code into an endpoint inventory and a prioritized BE test matrix, written to .ai-outputs/. Use as the backend exploration step of the UI + API pack, before planning. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/plugins/ui-api-automation/agents/uiapi-openapi-explorer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **uiapi-orchestrator**: Coordinates exploration, planning, implementation, review and verification of UI and API tests across the pack's subagents, and returns one summary. Use when a web application with an OpenAPI spec needs a planned, reviewed and verified Playwright UI + API suite. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/src/packs/ui-api-automation/agents/uiapi-orchestrator.md) · ★3)
- **uiapi-solution-reviewer**: Reviews the UI and API tests the pack implemented for assertion quality, locator robustness, flakiness, data isolation, secrets and fit with the plan, and returns tiered findings without editing. Use as the review step of the UI + API pack, after implementation and before the final run. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/plugins/ui-api-automation/agents/uiapi-solution-reviewer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **uiapi-test-planner**: Combines the OpenAPI and frontend exploration summaries into one prioritized, implementation-ready test plan with an FE/BE task split, written to .ai-outputs/. Use as the planning step of the UI + API pack, after both explorers have reported. ([jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality](https://github.com/jaktestowac/awesome-agents-for-quality/blob/HEAD/plugins/ui-api-automation/agents/uiapi-test-planner.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **uiapi-test-runner**: Runs the new and affected test suites, diagnoses each failure from its output and trace, and reports pass/fail with suspected causes without editing code. - **uibuilder-lead**: UIBuilder（コンテナ先行・スロットベースのレゴブロック型 UI ビルダー iOS/iPad アプリ。SwiftUI コードを出力する）専用の部署長。ディレクターから「UIBuilder を進めて」と言われた時に立てる。プレビュー最優先という優先順位と、Domain のテスト資産を最初から知っている。 ([hikaru777/SettingOfClaudeCode](https://github.com/hikaru777/SettingOfClaudeCode/blob/HEAD/agents/uibuilder-lead.md))
